dir=\"auto\"\u003e\"\u003cem\u003eI am not afraid of storms, for I am learning how to sail my ship.\u003c/em\u003e\"\u003cbr\u003e\n– Louisa May Alcott\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://marin.community\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eMarin\u003c/a\u003e is a research program, software platform, and community for the research and development of \u003ca href=\"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Foundation_model\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003efoundation models\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMarin's concern is training large language models. This includes data curation, transformation, filtering, tokenization, pretraining, posttraining, and evaluation. Beyond the artifacts, software, and infrastructure, behind these models, Marin is committed to openly sharing \u003cem\u003eall\u003c/em\u003e of the process knowledge required to build these models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMarin's core value is \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://openathena.ai/blog/open-development-of-frontier-ai/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eopen development\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e. We document our processes, experiments, and decisions as they happen. Every step, from raw data to the final model, is recorded. Failed experiments are part of that record.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMarin has also been used for building \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin/issues/1699\" data-hovercard-type=\"issue\" data-hovercard-url=\"/marin-community/marin/issues/1699/hovercard\"\u003eaudio-text models\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna\"\u003eDNA\u003c/a\u003e, and \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/Open-Athena/MarinFold\"\u003eprotein models\u003c/a\u003e. We encourage this work through the use of Marin as a library, in \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin-experiments\"\u003emarin/experiments\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://openathena.ai/blog/delphi/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eDelphi\u003c/a\u003e is Marin's open scaling suite scaling a LLM recipe from 3e18 to 1e23 FLOPs, inspired by \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/eleutherai/pythia\"\u003ePythia\u003c/a\u003e. It has three parts: a scaling recipe that maps compute budgets to model configurations, a scaling suite trained from that recipe on the Google TPU Research Cloud, and a scaling law that uses the smaller Delphi models to predict the larger ones.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWe released:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCheckpoints\u003c/strong\u003e for every run, available on Hugging Face at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/collections/marin-community/delphi-69f93cbd09845c03b070bae9\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emarin-community/delphi\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTraining mixture pipelines\u003c/strong\u003e that deterministically reproduce the mix from the public Nemotron-CC, StarCoderData, and ProofPile 2 in the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin/blob/67099b9aa5aa468155f0ce430276be72b39a3bd2/experiments/pretraining_datasets/nemotron.py\"\u003eMarin repo\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecipe code\u003c/strong\u003e as a forkable \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin/blob/78ae89a5324e09ddd9d0bc39af8565da40cfb3e9/experiments/scaling_law_sweeps/completed_adamh.py#L96\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eCompletedAdamHParams\u003c/code\u003e class\u003c/a\u003e in the Marin repo\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDevelopment methodology\u003c/strong\u003e as the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin/blob/main/docs/recipes/add_scaling_heuristic.md\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003eadd_scaling_heuristic\u003c/code\u003e agent skill\u003c/a\u003e in the Marin repo\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePlot-ready data\u003c/strong\u003e for the Delphi figures, with one config per figure and a \u003ccode\u003ewandb_url\u003c/code\u003e on every row, at \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/marin-community/delphi-blog-data\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emarin-community/delphi-blog-data\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMarin experiments are defined as a set of steps that can depend on each other and are executed in a topological order,\nlike a Makefile.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAs a brief example of how you can use Marin, here is a complete script for training a tiny model on \u003ca href=\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/roneneldan/TinyStories\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eTinyStories\u003c/a\u003e.\nYou can check out the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/marin-community/marin/blob/main/experiments/tutorials/train_tiny_model.py\"\u003efull script\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from fray.cluster import ResourceConfig\nfrom levanter.optim import AdamConfig\nfrom marin.execution.lazy import lower\nfrom marin.execution.step_runner import StepRunner\nfrom marin.experiment.data import tokenized\nfrom marin.experiment.train import train_lm\n\nfrom experiments.llama import llama_nano\nfrom experiments.marin_tokenizer import marin_tokenizer\n\n# 1. Tokenize the dataset as a lazy handle — nothing downloads yet.\ntinystories_tokenized = tokenized(\n name=\u0026quot;tokenized/tinystories\u0026quot;,\n source=\u0026quot;roneneldan/TinyStories\u0026quot;,\n tokenizer=marin_tokenizer,\n sample_count=1000, # cap at 1 000 samples per shard to keep the tutorial fast\n)\n\n# 2. Train the model — depends on the tokenized dataset above.\nnano_tinystories_model = train_lm(\n name=\u0026quot;checkpoints/marin-nano-tinystories\u0026quot;,\n version=\u0026quot;v1\u0026quot;,\n model=llama_nano,\n optimizer=AdamConfig(learning_rate=6e-4, weight_decay=0.1),\n # Steps can depend on other steps: nano_tinystories_model depends on tinystories_tokenized\n datasets={tinystories_tokenized: 1.0},\n batch_size=4,\n seq_len=2048,\n num_train_steps=100,\n z_loss_weight=None,\n evals=None, # no point evaluating such a tiny model\n resources=ResourceConfig.with_cpu(),\n)\n\nif __name__ == \u0026quot;__main__\u0026quot;:\n StepRunner().run([lower(nano_tinystories_model)])\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003efray\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecluster\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eResourceConfig\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elevanter\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eoptim\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAdamConfig\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexecution\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elazy\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elower\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexecution\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003estep_runner\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eStepRunner\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexperiment\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003edata\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenized\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexperiment\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etrain\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etrain_lm\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexperiments\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellama\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellama_nano\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eexperiments\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin_tokenizer\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin_tokenizer\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# 1. Tokenize the dataset as a lazy handle — nothing downloads yet.\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etinystories_tokenized\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etokenized\u003c/span\u003e(\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"tokenized/tinystories\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003esource\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"roneneldan/TinyStories\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenizer\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emarin_tokenizer\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003esample_count\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1000\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# cap at 1 000 samples per shard to keep the tutorial fast\u003c/span\u003e\n)\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# 2. Train the model — depends on the tokenized dataset above.\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003enano_tinystories_model\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etrain_lm\u003c/span\u003e(\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"checkpoints/marin-nano-tinystories\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eversion\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"v1\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ellama_nano\u003c/span\u003e,\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eoptimizer\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eAdamConfig\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elearning_rate\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan TESTING.md mkdocs.yml mkdocs.yml pyproject.toml pyproject.toml uv.lock uv.lock View all files Repository files navigation README Code of conduct Contributing Apache-2.0 license More items Marin " I am not afraid of storms, for I am learning how to sail my ship. " – Louisa May Alcott Marin is a research program, software platform, and community for the research and development of foundation models . Marin's concern is training large language models. This includes data curation, transformation, filtering, tokenization, pretraining, posttraining, and evaluation. Beyond the artifacts, software, and infrastructure, behind these models, Marin is committed to openly sharing all of the process knowledge required to build these models. Marin's core value is open development . We document our processes, experiments, and decisions as they happen. Every step, from raw data to the final model, is recorded. Failed experiments are part of that record. Marin has also been used for building audio-text models , DNA , and protein models . We encourage this work through the use of Marin as a library, in marin/experiments . Current work Frontier mixture-of-experts Our current focus is pretraining, from scratch, and posttraining a large (5e24 model-FLOPs, 500 billion+ total parameters) mixture-of-experts model to succeed on tasks of importance to scientists and researchers. Scaling suite Delphi is Marin's open scaling suite scaling a LLM recipe from 3e18 to 1e23 FLOPs, inspired by Pythia . It has three parts: a scaling recipe that maps compute budgets to model configurations, a scaling suite trained from that recipe on the Google TPU Research Cloud, and a scaling law that uses the smaller Delphi models to predict the larger ones. We released: Checkpoints for every run, available on Hugging Face at marin-community/delphi Training mixture pipelines that deterministically reproduce the mix from the public Nemotron-CC, StarCoderData, and ProofPile 2 in the Marin repo Recipe code as a forkable CompletedAdamHParams class in the Marin repo Development methodology as the add_scaling_heuristic agent skill in the Marin repo Plot-ready data for the Delphi figures, with one config per figure and a wandb_url on every row, at marin-community/delphi-blog-data Progress was tracked in GitHub issue #1337 . Other learnings Some additional consolidated learnings can be found on the Open Athena blog . A selection, below: Cluster Scheduling with Iris · scheduling jobs across heterogeneous clusters Improving our LLM Pretraining Efficiency · squeezing more throughput from pretraining Scaling Laws That Extrapolate 300× Past the Fit (Delphi) · predicting big models from small Mixture of Experts Quantile Balancing: Validated at 32B-A5B (1e22 FLOPs) Scale · keeping MoE experts load balanced Other models Previously, we used Marin to train an 8B parameter model that outperformed Llama 3.1 8B on our base-model benchmark suite . You can see the training script or read the retrospective . We also trained Marin 32B . Learning more &amp; using Marin The documentation for Marin is available on ReadTheDocs or in the docs/ folder. To get started with Marin: Install Marin. Train a tiny language model using Marin. See how to run a much larger DCLM 1B/1x experiment using Marin. See a summary of the experiments we've run. Join the Marin Discord to chat with the community. Example Marin experiments are defined as a set of steps that can depend on each other and are executed in a topological order, like a Makefile. As a brief example of how you can use Marin, here is a complete script for training a tiny model on TinyStories . You can check out the full script for more details. from fray . cluster import ResourceConfig from levanter . optim import AdamConfig from marin . execution . lazy import lower from marin . execution . step_runner import StepRunner from marin . experiment . data import tokenized from marin . experiment . train import train_lm from experiments . llama import llama_nano from experiments . marin_tokenizer import marin_tokenizer # 1. Tokenize the dataset as a lazy handle — nothing downloads yet. tinystories_tokenized = tokenized ( name = "tokenized/tinystories" , source = "roneneldan/TinyStories" , tokenizer = marin_tokenizer , sample_count = 1000 , # cap at 1 000 samples per shard to keep the tutorial fast ) # 2. Train the model — depends on the tokenized dataset above. nano_tinystories_model = train_lm ( name = "checkpoints/marin-nano-tinystories" , version = "v1" , model = llama_nano , optimizer = AdamConfig ( learning_rate = 6e-4 , weight_decay = 0.1 ), # Steps can depend on other steps: nano_tinystories_model depends on tinystories_tokenized datasets = { tinystories_tokenized : 1.0 }, batch_size = 4 , seq_len = 2048 , num_train_steps = 100 , z_loss_weight = None , evals = None , # no point evaluating such a tiny model resources = ResourceConfig . with_cpu (), ) if __name__ == "__main__" : StepRunner (). run ([ lower ( nano_tinystories_model )]) Here, we create two steps, one for tokenizing the dataset and one for training the model. The training step depends on the tokenized dataset step, so it will be executed after the tokenization step is completed. With slight modifications, you can extend this to train a larger model on a larger dataset , a mixture of datasets , even scaling to very large GPU or TPU pods (or multislice TPUs!). For Contributors See CONTRIBUTING.md for project workflow. See .agents/skills/ (also .claude/skills/ ) for loadable agent skills. For example, .agents/skills/add-dataset/ has a step-by-step guide to adding new datasets. Core Contributors Marin's core collaborators come from Stanford CRFM and Open Athena . dir=\"auto\"\u003eCompile PHP source code into native machine code ahead of time — producing\nnative executables, PHP extensions, and shared libraries — while keeping\nthe PHP syntax you already know.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/swoole/typephp/actions/workflows/linux-x64.yml\"\u003e\u003cimg dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat is TypePHP?\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-what-is-typephp\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What is TypePHP?\" href=\"#what-is-typephp\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypePHP is an Ahead-Of-Time (AOT) compiler that translates PHP source code into\nC++ and then into native machine code. Unlike a bytecode cache or a VM, it does\nnot interpret opcodes at runtime: it generates optimized native binaries that\nrun directly on the CPU.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt keeps familiar PHP syntax and adds compile-time type information, so the\ncompiler can emit fast, statically-typed C++ for hot paths. Dynamic PHP values,\ninternal functions, reflection, and object metadata continue to interoperate\nwith the Zend runtime through PHPX; user functions are not executed as Zend\nopcodes after they have been compiled.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypePHP is \u003cstrong\u003ewritten entirely in PHP\u003c/strong\u003e and is \u003cstrong\u003efully self-hosting\u003c/strong\u003e: the \u003ccode\u003etpc\u003c/code\u003e\ncompiler binary is built by compiling the compiler's own PHP source code with\nTypePHP. The bootstrap chain is pure PHP — no C or C++ glue in the compiler\nitself.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypePHP is under active development. It intentionally supports a defined,\ntestable subset of PHP rather than claiming drop-in compatibility with every\ndynamic PHP program. Read \u003ca href=\"#compatibility-model\"\u003eCompatibility model\u003c/a\u003e and the\n\u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/INCOMPATIBLE_PHP_FEATURES.md\"\u003eincompatible-feature list\u003c/a\u003e before adopting\nit for an existing application.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHow it works\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-how-it-works\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: How it works\" href=\"#how-it-works\"\u003e\u003csvg validate, and collect declarations\n │\n ▼\n lower function bodies and constants to C++17\n │\n ▼\n native compiler + reusable object/PCH caches\n │\n ▼\n executable | PHP extension | shared library | WASI component\"\u003e\u003cpre lang=\"text\" class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ePHP source + .stub.php declarations + optional C/C++ sources\n │\n ▼\n parse, validate, and collect declarations\n │\n ▼\n lower function bodies and constants to C++17\n │\n ▼\n native compiler + reusable object/PCH caches\n │\n ▼\n executable | PHP extension | shared library | WASI component\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe prepare phase builds the complete symbol model without allocating runtime\ncache IDs. Constants and declaration defaults retain their AST until the\nconvert phase, where they are lowered after all project symbols are known.\nThis two-phase design keeps multi-file and self-hosted builds deterministic.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFeatures\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-features\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Features\" href=\"#features\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSelf-hosting, written in PHP\u003c/strong\u003e — the TypePHP compiler is implemented\nentirely in PHP and bootstraps itself: \u003ccode\u003etpc\u003c/code\u003e compiles the compiler's own\nsource into a native binary.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTrue AOT compilation\u003c/strong\u003e — PHP is lowered to C++17, then to native machine\ncode. No interpreter, no opcode cache, no JIT warm-up.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eThree native build modes\u003c/strong\u003e — build a native \u003ccode\u003ebin\u003c/code\u003e executable, a loadable\nPHP \u003ccode\u003eext\u003c/code\u003e extension, or a reusable \u003ccode\u003elib\u003c/code\u003e shared library from the same codebase.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNative type system\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003eint\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003efloat\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003ebool\u003c/code\u003e map directly to C++\nscalar types (\u003ccode\u003eint64_t\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003edouble\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ebool\u003c/code\u003e) for orders-of-magnitude speedups\non numeric code.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHigh-precision numerics\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003ebigInt\u003c/code\u003e (GMP), \u003ccode\u003edecimal\u003c/code\u003e (libmpdec), and\n\u003ccode\u003ebigFloat\u003c/code\u003e (MPFR), with typed operators and method APIs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStrongly-typed containers\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003estd::array\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estd::vector\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estd::map\u003c/code\u003e, and\n\u003ccode\u003estd::ordered_map\u003c/code\u003e with compile-time element types; up to \u003cstrong\u003e10×\u003c/strong\u003e faster than\nPHP arrays and on par with C++ \u003ccode\u003estd::vector\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUniversal methods\u003c/strong\u003e — call methods directly on primitives\n(\u003ccode\u003e$s-\u0026gt;upper()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e$arr-\u0026gt;contains()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e$big-\u0026gt;mul(2)\u003c/code\u003e); statically-known calls\nare resolved directly at compile time.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMixed C++ / PHP\u003c/strong\u003e — call C++ functions from PHP (and vice versa) for\nperformance-critical kernels.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCompile-time functions \u0026amp; keywords\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003eany()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003erefval()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eobjval()\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003eexpected()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eunexpected()\u003c/code\u003e, plus \u003ccode\u003etoInt()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etoString()\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003etoArray()\u003c/code\u003e and\nfriends.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCompile-time safety\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003e#[Immutable]\u003c/code\u003e read-only contracts and \u003ccode\u003e#[ArrayDef]\u003c/code\u003e\narray-shape metadata, checked at compile time with zero runtime cost.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCompile-time code generation\u003c/strong\u003e — \u003ccode\u003e#[Getter]\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e#[Setter]\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e#[With]\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003e#[Constructor]\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e#[Printer]\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e#[Arrayable]\u003c/code\u003e generate type-safe methods\nfrom property declarations.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eModern PHP support\u003c/strong\u003e — PHP 8.4 property hooks, asymmetric visibility,\nPHP 8.5 \u003ccode\u003eclone()\u003c/code\u003e-with, and \u003ccode\u003e(void)\u003c/code\u003e discard expressions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCross-platform \u0026amp; WASM\u003c/strong\u003e — Linux, Windows, and macOS targets for x64 and\nARM64, plus WASI 0.2 and browser (Jco) output.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePython bridge\u003c/strong\u003e — generate IDE helpers for Python modules and convert\nPython scripts to TypePHP.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhy TypePHP?\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-why-typephp\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why TypePHP?\" href=\"#why-typephp\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTypePHP AOT\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eOpcode cache (OPcache)\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eJIT (PHP 8+)\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCompilation target\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNative machine code\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBytecode\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMachine code (trace)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStartup / warm-up\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNone (already compiled)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePer-process warm-up\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eJIT warm-up\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eType-driven optimization\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCompile-time, full-program\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNone\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLimited, trace-based\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNative executable output\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSource code protection\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCompiled to machine code\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBytecode (reversible)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBytecode (reversible)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDeterministic performance\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStrengths over plain PHP:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNear-native performance.\u003c/strong\u003e Numeric and container-heavy hot paths compile\ndown to the same machine code a C++ program would produce. See the\n\u003ca href=\"#benchmark\"\u003ebenchmark\u003c/a\u003e below.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSource protection.\u003c/strong\u003e Your source is compiled away — shipped artifacts are\nnative binaries, not readable PHP files.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNative process entry.\u003c/strong\u003e Binary mode starts directly from a native\nexecutable and does not require the PHP CLI or a separate interpreter\nprocess. The executable still embeds/links PHPX, \u003ccode\u003elibphp\u003c/code\u003e, and any configured\nnative libraries, which must be available in the deployment package.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGradual typing that actually pays off.\u003c/strong\u003e Add \u003ccode\u003euse native_types\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003estd::\u003c/code\u003e\ncontainers, and type declarations only where performance matters; the rest\nstays ordinary PHP.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eZend ecosystem interop.\u003c/strong\u003e Extension mode loads as a standard PHP extension,\nand projects can call supported internal functions and require other Zend\nextensions explicitly.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eRequirements\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-requirements\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Requirements\" href=\"#requirements\"\u003e\u003csvg dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePHP 8.4 – 8.5\u003c/strong\u003e CLI, development headers, and \u003ccode\u003ephp-config\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe matching \u003cstrong\u003ePHP embed library\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003elibphp.so\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003elibphp.dylib\u003c/code\u003e) for binary/shared-library\nbuilds on Unix-like systems\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGCC 9+\u003c/strong\u003e (or Clang) with \u003cstrong\u003eC++17\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCMake 3.24+\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComposer 2\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eHigh-precision math libraries: \u003cstrong\u003eGMP\u003c/strong\u003e, \u003cstrong\u003eMPFR\u003c/strong\u003e (libmpdec is bundled with PHPX)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Ubuntu/Debian\nsudo apt install build-essential cmake pkg-config libgmp-dev libmpfr-dev\n\n# RHEL/CentOS/Fedora\nsudo dnf install gcc gcc-c++ cmake pkgconf-pkg-config gmp-devel mpfr-devel\n\n# Arch Linux\nsudo pacman -S base-devel cmake pkgconf gmp mpfr\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Ubuntu/Debian\u003c/span\u003e\nsudo apt install build-essential cmake pkg-config libgmp-dev libmpfr-dev\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e RHEL/CentOS/Fedora\u003c/span\u003e\nsudo dnf install gcc gcc-c++ cmake pkgconf-pkg-config gmp-devel mpfr-devel\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Arch Linux\u003c/span\u003e\nsudo pacman -S base-devel cmake pkgconf gmp mpfr\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGMP powers \u003ccode\u003ebigInt\u003c/code\u003e and MPFR powers \u003ccode\u003ebigFloat\u003c/code\u003e. The \u003ccode\u003edecimal\u003c/code\u003e type is backed\nby libmpdec, which is bundled with PHPX — no separate install required.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eLinux x64 is the primary development and full-test CI platform. The compiler\nalso has Windows, macOS, ARM64, and WASI backends; availability of PHP embed,\ntoolchain, and third-party libraries still determines which target can be\nbuilt on a given host.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNative release assets are built with the latest PHP 8.5 ZTS release. TypePHP\npublishes Linux x64, Linux ARM64, macOS ARM64, and Windows x64 packages. Native\nNTS and 32-bit x86 packages are not provided. Linux and macOS archives contain\nthe compiler and production Composer dependencies, while the Windows archive\ncontains the complete matching PHP/PHPX runtime and SDK.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBinary and shared-library builds require PHP's \u003ccode\u003eembed\u003c/code\u003e SAPI. If \u003ccode\u003elibphp.so\u003c/code\u003e is\nmissing on Linux, \u003ccode\u003etpc.php\u003c/code\u003e can interactively download the PHP source and build\nit for you. A PHP extension build resolves Zend symbols from the host SAPI and\nmust not load a second \u003ccode\u003elibphp\u003c/code\u003e. See\n\u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/LIBPHP_INSTALLER.md\"\u003eAutomatic libphp.so build\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" ...\"\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBinary mode requires a global \u003ccode\u003emain()\u003c/code\u003e function. It may be declared with no\nparameters, or as \u003ccode\u003emain(int $argc, array $argv)\u003c/code\u003e to receive command-line\narguments, and must return \u003ccode\u003evoid\u003c/code\u003e. Top-level executable statements are not\nallowed; executable code belongs in a function or method.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" \u003ccode\u003e--mode\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eMode\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eFlag\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eOutput\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eNeeds \u003ccode\u003emain()\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTypical use\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBinary\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-m bin\u003c/code\u003e (default)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExecutable\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eYes\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCLI tools, long-running services, standalone apps\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eExtension\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-m ext\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePHP \u003ccode\u003e.so\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e.dll\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLoading compiled functions/classes into a PHP SAPI\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eLibrary\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-m lib\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShared library plus generated \u003ccode\u003e.stub.php\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNo\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReusing a compiled TypePHP API from another project\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Binary (default)\nbin/tpc.php app.php -o myapp\n\n# PHP extension\nbin/tpc.php extension/ -m ext -o my_extension\n\n# Shared library; also generates mylib.stub.php\nbin/tpc.php lib/ -m lib -o mylib\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e extension/ -m ext -o my_extension\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Shared library; also generates mylib.stub.php\u003c/span\u003e\nbin/tpc.php lib/ \u003ccode\u003eproject.yml\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-yaml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"name: myapp\nmode: bin\nphp-version: \u0026quot;8.5\u0026quot;\noptimize: 2\njob: 8\nbuild-dir: build\ncxx-std: c++17\n\nsources:\n - src\n - cpp-src\n - path: src/php85\n if: PHP_VERSION_ID \u0026gt;= 80500\n - path: src/windows\n if: PHP_OS_FAMILY == \u0026quot;Windows\u0026quot;\n\nignore:\n - src/experimental\n\ninclude-paths:\n - native/include\ndefines:\n - FEATURE_FAST_PATH=1\nlink-paths:\n - native/lib\nlink-libs:\n - curl\n\n# Zend extension requirements, not native linker libraries.\n# `extension-dependencies` is the equivalent long name; do not use both.\next-deps:\n - pdo_mysql\n - curl\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003emyapp\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003emode\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ebin\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ephp-version\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e8.5\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eoptimize\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ejob\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e8\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ebuild-dir\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ebuild\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003ecxx-std\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ec++17\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003esources\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003esrc\u003c/span\u003e\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecpp-src\u003c/span\u003e\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003epath\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003esrc/php85\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ePHP_VERSION_ID \u0026gt;= 80500\u003c/span\u003e\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003epath\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003esrc/windows\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ePHP_OS_FAMILY == \"Windows\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eignore\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003esrc/experimental\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003einclude-paths\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003enative/include\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003edefines\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eFEATURE_FAST_PATH=1\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003elink-paths\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003enative/lib\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003elink-libs\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecurl\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Zend extension requirements, not native linker libraries.\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e `extension-dependencies` is the equivalent long name; do not use both.\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eext-deps\u003c/span\u003e:\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003epdo_mysql\u003c/span\u003e\n - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ecurl\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePaths are resolved relative to the YAML file. A source entry may be a file or\ndirectory; conditional entries support \u003ccode\u003ePHP_VERSION\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ePHP_VERSION_ID\u003c/code\u003e, and\n\u003ccode\u003ePHP_OS_FAMILY\u003c/code\u003e. CLI arguments override their YAML counterparts. Native linker\ndependencies belong in \u003ccode\u003elink-libs\u003c/code\u003e; \u003ccode\u003eext-deps\u003c/code\u003e writes \u003ccode\u003eZEND_MOD_REQUIRED\u003c/code\u003e\nentries so Zend can reject loading when a required PHP extension is missing.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe build directory contains generated C++, dependency objects, and the\nprecompiled-header cache. Reusing it makes incremental builds much faster;\nuse \u003ccode\u003e--force\u003c/code\u003e only when the reusable PHPX objects must be rebuilt.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/COMPILER_CLI.md\"\u003eCompiler CLI\u003c/a\u003e for all project keys and command-line\nprecedence rules.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCompatibility model\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-compatibility-model\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Compatibility model\" href=\"#compatibility-model\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypePHP follows PHP syntax and runtime behavior where they are compatible with\nahead-of-time compilation, but it also makes several deliberate restrictions:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eglobal scope is declaration-only; executable statements must be inside a\nfunction or method;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ebinary mode has a strict \u003ccode\u003emain()\u003c/code\u003e signature;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003euse native_types\u003c/code\u003e opts scalar declarations into fixed native storage, so a\nvalue cannot later change to an incompatible type;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003estatically-known calls and properties are compiled directly, while supported\ndynamic operations use PHPX/Zend runtime fallbacks;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.stub.php\u003c/code\u003e files declare C++ or imported-library APIs and must contain empty\nbodies; \u003ccode\u003e#[Native]\u003c/code\u003e classes are not permitted in stub files;\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003esome highly dynamic reference, declaration, closure, and reflection patterns\nremain intentionally unsupported.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe compatibility boundary is part of the public contract and has both\npositive and negative tests. Consult\n\u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/INCOMPATIBLE_PHP_FEATURES.md\"\u003eIncompatible PHP features\u003c/a\u003e for the current,\nspecific list instead of assuming that absence from this README means support.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCompile-time attributes and code generation\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-compile-time-attributes-and-code-generation\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Compile-time attributes and code generation\" href=\"#compile-time-attributes-and-code-generation\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypePHP consumes its built-in code-generation attributes while lowering the\nclass. The generated methods retain the declared property types and take part\nin the same conflict, inheritance, and final-method checks as explicitly\ndeclared methods.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eAttribute\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTarget\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eGenerated API\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Getter]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInstance property, including a promoted property\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic function getName(): T\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Setter]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMutable instance property, including a promoted property\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic function setName(T $name): void\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[With]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMutable instance property, including a promoted property\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic function withName(T $name): static\u003c/code\u003e; clones the object, updates the clone, and returns it\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Constructor]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDeclared instance property\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAdds the property to a generated public \u003ccode\u003e__construct()\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Printer]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNamed class\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic function __toString(): string\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Arrayable]\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eNamed class\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic function toArray(): array\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-html-php notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026lt;?php\n\n#[Printer(fields: ['id', 'name'])]\n#[Arrayable(fields: ['id', 'name'])]\nfinal class User\n{\n #[Constructor, Getter, With]\n public int $id;\n\n #[Constructor, Getter, Setter]\n public string $name = 'guest';\n}\n\nfunction main(): void\n{\n $user = new User(7);\n $user-\u0026gt;setName('Alice');\n\n $copy = $user-\u0026gt;withId(8);\n echo $user-\u0026gt;getId(); // 7\n echo $copy-\u0026gt;getId(); // 8\n echo $user; // User(id=7, name=Alice)\n echo $user-\u0026gt;toArray()['name'];\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026lt;?php\u003c/span\u003e\n\n#[Printer(fields: [\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eid\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e])]\n#[Arrayable(fields: [\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eid\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e])]\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efinal\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eclass\u003c/span\u003e User\n{\n #[Constructor, Getter, With]\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003epublic\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eint\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eid\u003c/span\u003e;\n\n #[Constructor, Getter, Setter]\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003epublic\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003estring\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e = \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eguest\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e;\n}\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunction\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emain\u003c/span\u003e(): \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003evoid\u003c/span\u003e\n{\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e = \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003enew\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eUser\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e7\u003c/span\u003e);\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003esetName\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eAlice\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e);\n\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ecopy\u003c/span\u003e = \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ewithId\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e8\u003c/span\u003e);\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetId\u003c/span\u003e(); \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// 7\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ecopy\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetId\u003c/span\u003e(); \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// 8\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e; \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// User(id=7, name=Alice)\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003euser\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etoArray\u003c/span\u003e()[\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e'\u003c/span\u003e];\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWithout \u003ccode\u003efields\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e#[Printer]\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e#[Arrayable]\u003c/code\u003e use the class's own public\ninstance properties. The positional form, such as \u003ccode\u003e#[Arrayable(['id'])]\u003c/code\u003e, is\nequivalent to \u003ccode\u003e#[Arrayable(fields: ['id'])]\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e#[Getter]\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e#[Setter]\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e#[With]\u003c/code\u003e cannot target static properties or\nproperties with hooks. \u003ccode\u003e#[Setter]\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e#[With]\u003c/code\u003e additionally reject readonly\nproperties. \u003ccode\u003e#[Constructor]\u003c/code\u003e cannot be used when the class already declares\n\u003ccode\u003e__construct()\u003c/code\u003e, and required constructor properties must precede properties\nwith defaults. A generated method name that conflicts with a declared or\ninherited final method is a compile-time error.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eExamples\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-examples\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Examples\" href=\"#examples\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e1. Native types — compile-time numeric speedup\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-1-native-types--compile-time-numeric-speedup\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1. Native types — compile-time numeric speedup\" href=\"#1-native-types--compile-time-numeric-speedup\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-html-php notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026lt;?php\nuse native_types;\n\nfunction fib(int $n): int\n{\n if ($n == 1 || $n == 2) {\n return 1;\n }\n return fib($n - 1) + fib($n - 2);\n}\n\nfunction main(int $argc, array $argv): void\n{\n $n = (int)$argv[1];\n $begin = microtime(true);\n echo fib($n) . \u0026quot;\\n\u0026quot;;\n echo \u0026quot;Time: \u0026quot; . (microtime(true) - $begin) . \u0026quot;\\n\u0026quot;;\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026lt;?php\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003euse\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003enative_types\u003c/span\u003e;\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunction\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003efib\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eint\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e): \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eint\u003c/span\u003e\n{\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e == \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e || \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e == \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e) {\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ereturn\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e;\n }\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003ereturn\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003efib\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e - \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e) + \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003efib\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e - \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e);\n}\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunction\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emain\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eint\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eargc\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003earray\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eargv\u003c/span\u003e): \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003evoid\u003c/span\u003e\n{\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e = (\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003eint\u003c/span\u003e)\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eargv\u003c/span\u003e[\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e];\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ebegin\u003c/span\u003e = \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emicrotime\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e);\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003efib\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003en\u003c/span\u003e) . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e;\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eTime: \u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e . (\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emicrotime\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e) - \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ebegin\u003c/span\u003e) . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e;\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"bin/tpc.php fib.php -O3 -o fib\n./fib 30\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ebin/tpc.php fib.php -O3 -o fib\n./fib 30\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWith \u003ccode\u003euse native_types\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eint\u003c/code\u003e variables become C++ \u003ccode\u003eint64_t\u003c/code\u003e and arithmetic\ncompiles to plain CPU instructions instead of ZendVM calls.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. High-precision numerics\" href=\"#2-high-precision-numerics\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-html-php notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026lt;?php\ndeclare(strict_types=1);\nuse native_types;\n\nfunction main(): void\n{\n // 54-digit integer — automatically detected and stored as bigInt\n $a = std::bigInt(\u0026quot;123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234\u0026quot;);\n $b = std::bigInt(\u0026quot;987654321098765432109876543210987654321098765432109876\u0026quot;);\n\n echo $a-\u0026gt;add($b)-\u0026gt;toString() . \u0026quot;\\n\u0026quot;; // exact, no overflow\n\n // Exact decimal arithmetic — no binary floating-point error\n $c = std::decimal(\u0026quot;0.1\u0026quot;)-\u0026gt;add(std::decimal(\u0026quot;0.2\u0026quot;));\n echo $c-\u0026gt;toString() . \u0026quot;\\n\u0026quot;; // \u0026quot;0.3\u0026quot;\n\n // 256-bit floating point\n $pi = std::bigFloat(\u0026quot;3.14159265358979323846264338327950288419716939937510\u0026quot;);\n echo $pi-\u0026gt;mul(2)-\u0026gt;toString() . \u0026quot;\\n\u0026quot;;\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026lt;?php\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003edeclare\u003c/span\u003e(strict_types=\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e);\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003euse\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003enative_types\u003c/span\u003e;\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efunction\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emain\u003c/span\u003e(): \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003evoid\u003c/span\u003e\n{\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// 54-digit integer — automatically detected and stored as bigInt\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ea\u003c/span\u003e = std::\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ebigInt\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e123456789012345678901234567890123456789012345678901234\u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e);\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eb\u003c/span\u003e = std::\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ebigInt\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e987654321098765432109876543210987654321098765432109876\u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e);\n\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ea\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eadd\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003eb\u003c/span\u003e)-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etoString\u003c/span\u003e() . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e; \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// exact, no overflow\u003c/span\u003e\n\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Exact decimal arithmetic — no binary floating-point error\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ec\u003c/span\u003e = std::\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003edecimal\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e0.1\u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e)-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eadd\u003c/span\u003e(std::\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003edecimal\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e0.2\u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e));\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003ec\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etoString\u003c/span\u003e() . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e; \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// \"0.3\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// 256-bit floating point\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003epi\u003c/span\u003e = std::\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003ebigFloat\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e3.14159265358979323846264338327950288419716939937510\u003c/span\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e);\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003epi\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003emul\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e2\u003c/span\u003e)-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etoString\u003c/span\u003e() . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e;\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSee \u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/HIGH_PRECISION_TYPES.md\"\u003eHigh-precision types\u003c/a\u003e and\n\u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/NATIVE_TYPES.md\"\u003eNative types\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// strlen()\u003c/span\u003e\n \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eecho\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e$\u003c/span\u003es\u003c/span\u003e-\u0026gt;\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eupper\u003c/span\u003e() . \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\\n\"\u003c/span\u003e; s\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e~8×\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emicro_bench.php\u003c/code\u003e benchmarks measure core PHP language performance — function calls, object\nproperty access, array/hash access, string handling, control flow, and more.\nThe checked-in workloads are \u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/benchmark/bench.php\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebenchmark/bench.php\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e and\n\u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/benchmark/micro_bench.php\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebenchmark/micro_bench.php\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e. Additional focused\nperformance regressions live in the same \u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/benchmark\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebenchmark/\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/a\u003e directory.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese numbers are a project measurement snapshot, not a performance guarantee.\nPHP version, compiler, CPU, optimization flags, and enabled extensions can all\nchange the result; compare on the same machine with the same workload before\nmaking deployment decisions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" AOT)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e6.4 s\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eC++ \u003ccode\u003estd::vector\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e6.2 \u003cstrong\u003e10× faster\u003c/strong\u003e than PHP arrays and performs\nclose to the hand-written C++ result in this workload. highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Compile a single file\nbin/tpc.php app.php\n\n# Optimize and run, passing args to the program after `--`\nbin/tpc.php app.php -O3 -r -- --flag value\n\n# Compile a project defined in project.yml\nbin/tpc.php project.yml -O2 -j 8\n\n# Build a PHP extension\nbin/tpc.php extension/ -m ext -o my_extension\n\n# Only generate C++ (skip compile \u0026amp; link)\nbin/tpc.php app.php --dry --build-dir /tmp/typephp-build\n\n# Compile to WASI 0.2\nbin/tpc.php --wasm app.php\n\n# Compile for the browser (requires jco)\nbin/tpc.php --wasm=browser app.php\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Compile a single \u003ccode\u003e0\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-d\u003c/code\u003e, tracking\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-o\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--output \u0026lt;file\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOutput file name\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-m\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--mode \u0026lt;bin|lib|ext\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBuild mode (default \u003ccode\u003ebin\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-r\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--run\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRun after a successful build\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-j\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--job \u0026lt;num\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eParallel compile jobs (default \u003ccode\u003e4\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-f\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e--force\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRebuild reusable PHPX objects instead of using the cache\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--build-dir \u0026lt;dir\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDirectory for generated C++ and intermediates\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--dry\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGenerate C++ only, skip compile and link\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--php-version \u0026lt;8.4|8.5\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePHP syntax version to accept\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--cxx-std \u0026lt;ver\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eC++ standard (e.g. \u003ccode\u003ec++17\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ec++20\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--march \u0026lt;arch\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTarget instruction set (e.g. \u003ccode\u003enative\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--target-platform triple\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--lto\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnable link-time optimization\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--sanitize \u0026lt;type\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnable a sanitizer (e.g. \u003ccode\u003eaddress\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--profile\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnable Linux gperftools profiling\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--format\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFormat generated C++ with clang-format\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--no-literal-strings\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDisable the literal-string table optimization\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e--no-progress\u003c/code\u003e, controls\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e-I\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e-D\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e-L\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibphp.dylib\u003c/code\u003e is missing:\u003c/strong\u003e install/build the matching PHP embed SAPI, set\n\u003ccode\u003ePHP_HOME\u003c/code\u003e, or let \u003ccode\u003ebin/tpc.php\u003c/code\u003e offer the interactive Linux installer.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePHPX cannot be found:\u003c/strong\u003e set \u003ccode\u003ePHPX_HOME\u003c/code\u003e to a PHPX installation containing\n\u003ccode\u003einclude/\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003elib/libphpx.so\u003c/code\u003e (or the platform equivalent), then build PHPX\nbefore compiling the project.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eStartup crashes or ABI errors:\u003c/strong\u003e the PHP headers, \u003ccode\u003ephp-config\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003elibphp\u003c/code\u003e,\nand loaded extension ABI must agree on the PHP version and ZTS/NTS mode. Do\nnot mix artifacts from different PHP builds.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eIncremental builds are unexpectedly slow:\u003c/strong\u003e keep a stable \u003ccode\u003e--build-dir\u003c/code\u003e so\nobject and PCH caches can be reused. When an external test runner already\nruns several tests concurrently, avoid multiplying that concurrency by an\nunnecessarily large \u003ccode\u003etpc -j\u003c/code\u003e value.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA project compiles with \u003ccode\u003ebin/tpc.php\u003c/code\u003e but fails with \u003ccode\u003etpc\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/strong\u003e reproduce with\nthe self-hosted compiler. Bootstrap execution can expose dynamic-call or ABI\npaths that the PHP-hosted compiler does not exercise.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"composer install\nPHPX_HOME=/path/to/phpx vendor/bin/phpunit\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecomposer install\nPHPX_HOME=/path/to/phpx vendor/bin/phpunit\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePHPT is the end-to-end suite. Build the self-hosted compiler first and pass it\nexplicitly to the test runner; using the Zend PHP executable as \u003ccode\u003e--compiler\u003c/code\u003e\ndoes not test the deployed compiler:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"PHPX_HOME=/path/to/phpx php bin/tpc.php project.yml --job 2 --no-progress\nphp run-tests.php -q -j8 --compiler ./tpc tests/compiler\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ePHPX_HOME=/path/to/phpx php bin/tpc.php project.yml --job 2 --no-progress\nphp run-tests.php -q -j8 --compiler ./tpc tests/compiler\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStatic analysis and the source-derived coverage matrix are separate checks:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"composer analyse\nphp bin/analyze-test-coverage.php\nphp bin/analyze-test-coverage.php \\\n --format=markdown --output=build/test-coverage.md --strict\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003ecomposer analyse\nphp bin/analyze-test-coverage.php\nphp bin/analyze-test-coverage.php \\\n --format=markdown --output=build/test-coverage.md --strict\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe coverage tool reports PHP version × feature × positive compilation ×\nruntime semantics × negative diagnostics, plus concrete PHP-parser AST nodes.\nIt intentionally does not publish a single percentage without an explicit\ndenominator. See \u003ca href=\"/swoole/typephp/blob/master/docs/en/TEST_COVERAGE_ANALYZER.md\"\u003eTest coverage analyzer\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGitHub Actions runs PHPUnit and self-hosted PHPT on PHP 8.4 and 8.5. Changes to\ncompiler behavior should add a focused PHPUnit test for internal/code-generation\nrules and a PHPT whenever runtime output or diagnostics are observable.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv
